<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6532C137-9071-4123-8B77-BEABFCDB3BC7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is the difference, in cm, between $3.2 \ \text{metres}$ and $30 \ \text{cm}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}    &= 3.2 \ \text{m} - 30 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                     &= 3.2 \times 100 \ \text{cm} - 30 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                     &= 320 - 30   \\\\
                     &= 290  \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$290  \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}    &= 3.2 \ \text{m} - 30 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                     &= 3.2 \times 100 \ \text{cm} - 30 \ \text{cm} \\\\
                     &= 320 - 30   \\\\
                     &= 290  \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$290  \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$

</div>

\end{aligned}
$

\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \times 17 \\\\
&= \dfrac{15 \times 17 \times 2}{2} \\\\

&= \dfrac{255 \times 2}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{255 \times \cancel {2}}{\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 255
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7E1A8AB3-C593-4477-9220-607D3E76FDBE'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find the difference between $2.7 \ \text{metres}$ and $32 \ \text{centimetres}$.

Give your answer in metres.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
. 

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2.7 \ \text{m} - 32 \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 2.7 \ \text{m} - \dfrac{32}{100} \ \text{m} \\\\
&= 2.7 \ \text{m} - 0.32 \ \text{m} \\\\
&= 2.38 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2.38 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note. 

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2.7 \ \text{m} - 32 \ \text{cm} \\\\
&= 2.7 \ \text{m} - \dfrac{32}{100} \ \text{m} \\\\
&= 2.7 \ \text{m} - 0.32 \ \text{m} \\\\
&= 2.38 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2.38 \ \text{m}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Complete the table of values for this number machine with the rule 'multiply by $4$, then subtract $3$'.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Number IN $= 10$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10 \times 4 - 3 \\\\
&= 40 - 3 \\\\
&= 37
\end{aligned}
$

Number OUT $= 81$

Let input number $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times a - 3 &= 81 \\\\
4 \times a &= 81 + 3 \\\\
4 \times a &= 84 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{84}{4} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{21 \times \cancel 4}{\cancel 4} \\\\
a &= 21
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$37 \\\\ 21$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Number IN $= 10$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10 \times 4 - 3 \\\\
&= 40 - 3 \\\\
&= 37
\end{aligned}
$

Number OUT $= 81$

Let input number $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times a - 3 &= 81 \\\\
4 \times a &= 81 + 3 \\\\
4 \times a &= 84 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{84}{4} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{21 \times \cancel 4}{\cancel 4} \\\\
a &= 21
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the rule for this number machine? Write your answer inside the machine.

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the rule $= \text{Multiply by}\ a \ \text{then add}\ b$

Input $= 2$

Output $= 17$

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 2 + b &= 17 \\\\
2a + b &= 17
\end{aligned}
$

Input $= 7$

Output $= 52$

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 7 + b &= 52 \\\\
7a + b &= 52
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for $a$

$
\begin{aligned}
7a + b &= 52 \\\\
7a + (17 - 2a) &= 52 \\\\
7a - 2a &= 52 - 17 \\\\
5a &= 35 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{35}{5} \\\\
a &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for $b$

$
\begin{aligned}
2a + b &= 17 \\\\
2 \times 7 + b &= 17 \\\\
14 + b &= 17 \\\\
b &= 17 - 14 \\\\
b &= 3 \\\
\end{aligned}
$

Lets verify,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9 \times 7 + 3 \\\\
&= 63 + 3 \\\\
&= \green {66} = 66 \\\
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10 \times 7 + 3 \\\\
&= 70 + 3 \\\\
&= \green {73} = 73 \\\
\end{aligned}
$

Therefore, the rule $= \text{Multipy by}\ 7 \ \text{then add}\ 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\text{Multipy by}\ 7 \ \text{then add}\ 3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the rule $= \text{Multiply by}\ a \ \text{then add}\ b$

Input $= 2$

Output $= 17$

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 2 + b &= 17 \\\\
2a + b &= 17
\end{aligned}
$

Input $= 7$

Output $= 52$

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times 7 + b &= 52 \\\\
7a + b &= 52
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for $a$

$
\begin{aligned}
7a + b &= 52 \\\\
7a + (17 - 2a) &= 52 \\\\
7a - 2a &= 52 - 17 \\\\
5a &= 35 \\\\
a &= \dfrac{35}{5} \\\\
a &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

Lets solve for $b$

$
\begin{aligned}
2a + b &= 17 \\\\
2 \times 7 + b &= 17 \\\\
14 + b &= 17 \\\\
b &= 17 - 14 \\\\
b &= 3 \\\
\end{aligned}
$

Lets verify,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9 \times 7 + 3 \\\\
&= 63 + 3 \\\\
&= \green {66} = 66 \\\
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10 \times 7 + 3 \\\\
&= 70 + 3 \\\\
&= \green {73} = 73 \\\
\end{aligned}
$

Therefore, the rule $= \text{Multipy by}\ 7 \ \text{then add}\ 3$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Add the smallest of the following numbers to the largest: 

$1,  \quad 0.13,  \quad 0.8,  \quad 0.012,  \quad 1.238,  \quad 0.028$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Smallest}     &= 0.012 \\\\
\text{Largest}      &= 1.238 \\\\
\text{Sum}          &= 0.012 + 1.238 \\\\
                    &= 1.25
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Smallest}     &= 0.012 \\\\
\text{Largest}      &= 1.238 \\\\
\text{Sum}          &= 0.012 + 1.238 \\\\
                    &= 1.25
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

\boxed{\huge { \text{ 2 }}} \quad

\boxed{\huge { \text{ 4 }}}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-79688C18-5171-4E25-9231-AF07AF17DF2A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which of the following numbers is closest in value to $1$?

$1.1, \quad 0.988, \quad 1.009, \quad 0.99, \quad 1.01$
 
</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's calculate the difference between the number and $1$.

| Number   |     Difference       |
|:--------:|:--------------------:|
|   $1.1$  |  $1.1 - 1 = 0.1$   |
|  $0.988$ | $1 - 0.988 = 0.012$  |
|  $1.009$ | $1.009 - 1 = 0.009$  |
|  $0.99$  |  $1 - 0.99 = 0.01$   |
|  $1.01$  |  $1.01 - 1 = 0.01$   |

From the calculation above, the smallest difference is $0.009$.

Number closest in value $= 1.009$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.009$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's calculate the difference between the number and $1$.

| Number   |     Difference       |
|:--------:|:--------------------:|
|   $1.1$  |  $1.1 - 1 = 0.1$   |
|  $0.988$ | $1 - 0.988 = 0.012$  |
|  $1.009$ | $1.009 - 1 = 0.009$  |
|  $0.99$  |  $1 - 0.99 = 0.01$   |
|  $1.01$  |  $1.01 - 1 = 0.01$   |

From the calculation above, the smallest difference is $0.009$.

Number closest in value $= 1.009$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1.009$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

$9 \ \boxed{\phantom{555}} \ 4 = 25\ \boxed{\phantom{555}} \  5$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$9 \ \boxed{-} \ 4 = 25\ \boxed{\div} \  5$

Lets verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Left}       &= 9 - 4 \\\\
                  &= 5 \\\\
\text{Right}      &= 25 \div 5 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{25}{5} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{5 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{5 \times \cancel 5}{\cancel 5} \\\\
                  &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$-, \div$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$9 \ \boxed{-} \ 4 = 25\ \boxed{\div} \  5$

Lets verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Left}       &= 9 - 4 \\\\
                  &= 5 \\\\
\text{Right}      &= 25 \div 5 \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{25}{5} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{5 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{5 \times \cancel 5}{\cancel 5} \\\\
                  &= 5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subsubquestion'>
<div class='question subsubquestion'>

$32 \div ( 10 - \boxed{\phantom{5555}}) = 4$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
32 \div (10 - a)        &= 4 \\\\
\dfrac{32}{10 - a}      &= 4 \\\\
10 - a                  &= \dfrac{32}{4} \\\\
10 - a                  &= 8 \\\\
a                       &= 10 - 8 \\\\
a                       &= 2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>

<div class='question question'>

Factors are numbers that divide into another number exactly.
For example, the factors of $28$ are $1, 2, 4, 7, 14$ and $28$.


$28$ is called a **perfect number** because the 
factors of $28$, not including the number $28$ 
itself, add up to $28$.

So, $1 + 2 + 4 + 7 + 14 = 28$.

</div>

\text{Sum of factors of 12}   &= 1 + 2 + 3 + 4 \\\\
                              &= 10 \neq 12
\end{aligned}

</div>

12 is not a perfect number

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0EBD3316-5CD6-40EB-8B8C-D3B8C9C268B1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Neha won twelve thousand and eighteen pounds in a lottery. Jasmine 
won three hundred and nine pounds. How much did they win 
altogether? Give your answer in figures, not words.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total win}   &= 12018 + 309 \\\\
                    &= 12327
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12327$

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-630C101F-E5A2-4A98-AD35-AF1C60B9AD03'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Circle two numbers which have the same value.

$
8 \dfrac{1}{20} \quad 
\dfrac{17}{2}   \quad 
8.1             \quad 
8.05            \quad
8 \dfrac{1}{5}
$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's convert the numbers into decimals.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \dfrac {1} {20} \quad \dfrac {17} {2} \quad 8.1 \quad 8.05 \quad \dfrac {1} {5} \\\\
&= 8.05 \quad 8.5 \quad 8.1 \quad 8.05 \quad 0.2
\end{aligned}
$

Equal numbers $= 8 \dfrac {1} {20}, \quad 8.05$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8 \dfrac {1} {20}, \quad 8.05$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's convert the numbers into decimals.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 8 \dfrac {1} {20} \quad \dfrac {17} {2} \quad 8.1 \quad 8.05 \quad \dfrac {1} {5} \\\\
&= 8.05 \quad 8.5 \quad 8.1 \quad 8.05 \quad 0.2
\end{aligned}
$

Equal numbers $= 8 \dfrac {1} {20}, \quad 8.05$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$8 \dfrac {1} {20}, \quad 8.05$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
